<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Below are the deck lists from Start Deck 100, a 100-deck superset that released in Japan on December 17th. Each of the 100 decks is numbered. Although we can identify specific cards in the decks because of the cards found in the set, in cases where more than one card of the same name exists in the set, it is often impossible to determine which specific card has been included in a specific deck. As many as 40 new cards that appeared in these decks are expected to debut in the international set <em>Sword &amp; Shield—Brilliant Stars.</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Instead of being sorted by their usual order, deck lists below appear in the order they appear on their Japanese deck lists.</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="text-align:center;white-space:pre-wrap;">Rayquaza VMAX</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 17</strong><br>2 Vulpix FST 29<br>1 Ninetales FST 30 or FST 31<br>2 Torkoal BRS 23<br>1 Heatmor BST 26<br>3 Mareep EVS 54<br>2 Flaaffy CRE 48 or EVS 55<br>1 Emolga EVS 57 or ??? ???<br>3 Thundurus CRE 52<br>1 Rayquaza V EVS 110<br>1 Rayquaza VMAX EVS 111</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 25 </strong><br>2 Pokémon Catcher SSH 175<br>1 Poké Ball CPA 59<br>1 Team Yell’s Cheer BRS 149<br>2 Zinnia’s Resolve EVS 164<br>2 Energy Search SSH 161<br>2 Great Ball CPA 52<br>2 Ultra Ball BRS ???<br>3 Switch SSH 183<br>2 Shauna FST 240<br>1 Judge FST 235<br>4 Barry BRS 130<br>3 Professor’s Research BRS ???</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18 </strong><br>6 Fire Energy SWSHEnergy 2<br>12 Lightning Energy</p>
</div>
